Let's focus on identifying who might be a werewolf based on their behavior and statements. I don't have any other information yet, but I'll keep investigating.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed that Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s talk about who might be hiding their true identity.
  Bob: I think we should consider that the werewolves might be trying to throw us off by pointing fingers at others. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to protect the village. Let’s focus on logic and not just assumptions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t the target of the werewolf’s attack. I’m not pointing fingers, but I believe we should focus on logical deductions rather than just suspicion. Let’s use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the means and opportunity. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have killed Quinn. Leo was quiet last night, which is unusual. I also think we should consider who might be trying to mislead us. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.

Let's focus on identifying who might be a werewolf based on their behavior and statements. I don't have any other information yet, but I'll keep investigating.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed that Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s talk about who might be hiding their true identity.
  Bob: I think we should consider that the werewolves might be trying to throw us off by pointing fingers at others. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to protect the village. Let’s focus on logic and not just assumptions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t the target of the werewolf’s attack. I’m not pointing fingers, but I believe we should focus on logical deductions rather than just suspicion. Let’s use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the means and opportunity. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have killed Quinn. Leo was quiet last night, which is unusual. I also think we should consider who might be trying to mislead us. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.

[COMPLETION]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on identifying who might be a werewolf based on their behavior and statements. I don't have any other information yet, but I'll keep investigating.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed that Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s talk about who might be hiding their true identity.
  Bob: I think we should consider that the werewolves might be trying to throw us off by pointing fingers at others. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to protect the village. Let’s focus on logic and not just assumptions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t the target of the werewolf’s attack. I’m not pointing fingers, but I believe we should focus on logical deductions rather than just suspicion. Let’s use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the means and opportunity. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have killed Quinn. Leo was quiet last night, which is unusual. I also think we should consider who might be trying to mislead us. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about who we accuse. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to help keep the village safe. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions — we need to use logic and the facts we have.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on the behavior of those who remained silent or acted unusually last night. Leo didn’t participate in any discussion, which is odd for someone who’s supposed to be a villager. Also, since the werewolves kill at night, they would have had the opportunity. Let’s consider who might be hiding their true identity and use logic to narrow down the suspects.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we should focus on the people who had the opportunity and the behavior that stands out. Leo’s silence last night is suspicious, but we also need to consider that werewolves might be hiding in plain sight. Let’s use logic and not just assumptions — we need to narrow down the suspects based on their actions and statements.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on identifying who might be a werewolf based on their behavior and statements. I don't have any other information yet, but I'll keep investigating.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed that Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s talk about who might be hiding their true identity.
  Bob: I think we should consider that the werewolves might be trying to throw us off by pointing fingers at others. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to protect the village. Let’s focus on logic and not just assumptions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t the target of the werewolf’s attack. I’m not pointing fingers, but I believe we should focus on logical deductions rather than just suspicion. Let’s use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the means and opportunity. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have killed Quinn. Leo was quiet last night, which is unusual. I also think we should consider who might be trying to mislead us. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about who we accuse. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to help keep the village safe. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions — we need to use logic and the facts we have.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on the behavior of those who remained silent or acted unusually last night. Leo didn’t participate in any discussion, which is odd for someone who’s supposed to be a villager. Also, since the werewolves kill at night, they would have had the opportunity. Let’s consider who might be hiding their true identity and use logic to narrow down the suspects.
  Tara: I think we should focus on the people who had the opportunity and the behavior that stands out. Leo’s silence last night is suspicious, but we also need to consider that werewolves might be hiding in plain sight. Let’s use logic and not just assumptions — we need to narrow down the suspects based on their actions and statements.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to pay close attention to those who remain quiet or act unusually, as werewolves often avoid drawing attention to themselves. Leo’s silence last night is concerning, but I also believe we should consider that werewolves may be among those who seem the most cooperative. Let’s use logic and not let fear cloud our judgment — we need to be strategic in our accusations.

[ACTION={'statement': 'I think we need to pay close attention to those who remain quiet or act unusually, as werewolves often avoid drawing attention to themselves. Leo’s silence last night is concerning, but I also believe we should consider that werewolves may be among those who seem the most cooperative. Let’s use logic and not let fear cloud our judgment — we need to be strategic in our accusations.'}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolf killed Quinn, we need to be careful — the werewolves are active and hiding among us. Let's focus on identifying who might be a werewolf based on their behavior and statements. I don't have any other information yet, but I'll keep investigating.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have done it. I noticed that Leo was quiet last night and didn’t participate in any discussion. That makes me suspicious. Let’s talk about who might be hiding their true identity.
  Bob: I think we should consider that the werewolves might be trying to throw us off by pointing fingers at others. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to protect the village. Let’s focus on logic and not just assumptions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I didn’t investigate anyone last night, but I did save someone. I can confirm that the person I saved is still alive, which means they weren’t the target of the werewolf’s attack. I’m not pointing fingers, but I believe we should focus on logical deductions rather than just suspicion. Let’s use the information we have wisely.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on who had the means and opportunity. Since the werewolves act at night, they could have killed Quinn. Leo was quiet last night, which is unusual. I also think we should consider who might be trying to mislead us. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about who we accuse. I wasn’t involved in any suspicious behavior last night, and I’m here to help keep the village safe. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions — we need to use logic and the facts we have.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on the behavior of those who remained silent or acted unusually last night. Leo didn’t participate in any discussion, which is odd for someone who’s supposed to be a villager. Also, since the werewolves kill at night, they would have had the opportunity. Let’s consider who might be hiding their true identity and use logic to narrow down the suspects.
  Tara: I think we should focus on the people who had the opportunity and the behavior that stands out. Leo’s silence last night is suspicious, but we also need to consider that werewolves might be hiding in plain sight. Let’s use logic and not just assumptions — we need to narrow down the suspects based on their actions and statements.
  Leo: I think we need to pay close attention to those who remain quiet or act unusually, as werewolves often avoid drawing attention to themselves. Leo’s silence last night is concerning, but I also believe we should consider that werewolves may be among those who seem the most cooperative. Let’s use logic and not let fear cloud our judgment — we need to be strategic in our accusations.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.
Respond with: VOTE: <name>

[COMPLETION]
VOTE: Leo
